- **assembly-contig-sd-representation-quantification**: Use when comparing how completely two de novo genome assembly versions represent annotated repeat/segmental duplication sequences, or when auditing whether an assembler upgrade improved recovery of known genomic sequence. - **assembly-contiguity-assessment**: Use when planning or evaluating de novo diploid genome assembly from 10x Chromium Linked-Read data, especially to choose fragment length (Mu_F) or physical coverage (CF) parameters and predict contig N50 outcomes at a fixed total coverage such as C = 56x. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-assessment))
- **assembly-contiguity-metric-computation**: Use when FASTA assemblies (contigs/scaffolds) from metagenome assemblers must be scored against a known reference to compare contiguity (NGA50), misassemblies, and base-level accuracy across tools or ablation modes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metric-computation--536a6c9d834dd60c))
- **assembly-contiguity-metric-computation**: Use when comparing multiple assemblies of the same genome (e.g. outputs from different assemblers such as SPAdes, Velvet, ABySS, MEGAHIT) and you need contiguity statistics like N50, N90, auN, or largest contig length to rank them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metric-computation--ca675f735db0bc41))
- **assembly-contiguity-metric-extraction**: Use when assemblies built from raw, corrected, or polished long reads (Miniasm or Flye) must be compared via reference-based metrics — NGA50, NGA75, genome coverage, errors per 100 kbp, misassemblies — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metric-extraction))
- **assembly-contiguity-metrics-comparison**: Decision support for choosing between cross-sample merged and individual assemblies when evaluating contig sets from multiple assemblers or related samples by contiguity metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metrics-comparison))
- **assembly-contiguity-metrics-computation**: Use when comparing diploid genome assemblies from long noisy reads (PacBio CLR, Nanopore R9/R10) and you need NG50, phase block NG50, Hamming error, BUSCO, or reference-based accuracy figures to reproduce published Tables 1-2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metrics-computation--7202f7152b8f6027))
- **assembly-contiguity-metrics-computation**: Use when you have ≥2 assemblies (contig FASTA files) — e.g. a modified assembler pipeline vs a baseline — and need reproducible contiguity (N50, longest contig) and quality (BUSCO, k-mer QV) comparisons on human long-read data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metrics-computation--57ca048d84476138))
- **assembly-contiguity-metrics-computation**: Reference for evaluating and comparing de novo short-read assemblies (e.g., SOAPdenovo2 vs SOAPdenovo1.05) on benchmark datasets such as GAGE (S. aureus, R. sphaeroides, human chr14) or Assemblathon1 using N50, correct assembly length, and error counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metrics-computation))
- **assembly-contiguity-metrics-evaluation**: Use when comparing genome assemblies from several assemblers on the same sample and you need reference-based QUAST metrics (contig count, genome fraction, NGA50, misassemblies, # mismatches/# indels per 100 kbp) plus wall-clock time and peak memory to rank the tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metrics-evaluation--6d09a687120176f8))
- **assembly-contiguity-metrics-evaluation**: Use when a hybrid or long-read assembly (backbone_raw.fasta, polished consensus) has been produced and you must check N50 before proceeding to consensus tuning or final reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-metrics-evaluation--46c6767ee38d780b))
- **assembly-contiguity-n50-evaluation**: Use when short-read metagenomic FASTQ inputs are being assembled within virMine and no single assembler is obviously appropriate, so contiguity (N50) should decide which assembly proceeds to annotation and viral scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-n50-evaluation--41cf211966ad5522))
- **assembly-contiguity-n50-evaluation**: Use when de novo assembled 10x Linked-Read (Supernova2) assemblies must be compared across parameter settings (total coverage C, physical coverage CF, read coverage per CR) using N50 and NA50 contiguity/accuracy metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-n50-evaluation--77c9fae18b5a6d39))
- **assembly-contiguity-ng50-evaluation**: Use when a strain-separated assembly (e.g. assembly.scaffolds.fa from Strainberry) must be scored against known reference genomes to quantify contiguity (NG50), base accuracy (ANI), and redundancy (duplication ratio). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-ng50-evaluation))
- **assembly-contiguity-nx-plot-comparison**: Use when multiple metagenome assemblers have been run on matched ONT long-read + Illumina paired-end samples and you need to quantify contiguity differences via Nx-length plots and scaffold categories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-nx-plot-comparison))
- **assembly-contiguity-quality-assessment**: Use when short-read and/or long-read metagenome samples have been assembled (short-read-only, long-read-only, or hybrid) and you must compare assembly contiguity across assemblers or dispatch modes before binning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-quality-assessment))
- **assembly-contiguity-statistics**: Use when you have a draft assembly FASTA (e.g. DBG2OLC's backbone_raw.fasta) and need N50/NG50 contiguity figures to decide whether to tune parameters, proceed to consensus, or compare against published metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-statistics))
- **assembly-contiguity-statistics**: Use when you have contigs from a MetaCortex (or similar de Bruijn graph) metagenomic assembly and need to quantify assembly quality as contig counts, N50 and maximum contig length after a length cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-contiguity-statistics))
- **assembly-continuity-metric-tabulation**: Use when comparing contiguity and binning yield of metagenome assemblies produced under different read-type (short vs hybrid) or grouping (sample-wise vs co-assembly) settings, using CAMISIM-style simulated time-series reads. - **assembly-cut-notes**: Produces structured editorial notes from an assembly cut description, identifying material gaps, sequence order issues, and structural foundations before the rough cut stage. ([ur-grue/autopunk-media-skills](https://github.com/ur-grue/autopunk-media-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tv-documentary/post-production/assembly-cut-notes) · ★46 · 2 copies)
- **assembly-definitions**: Assembly definition management — when to create asmdefs, reference rules, Editor/Runtime/Test separation, platform filters, compilation speed optimization. - **assembly-deposit-retrieval**: Use when a published genome assembly paper reports assembly statistics (N50, coverage) and you need to download the deposited FASTA files and independently reproduce those metrics. - **assembly-editor**: Editorial assembly specialist for Grok Imagine long-form productions. ([adunext/human-video-knowledge](https://github.com/adunext/human-video-knowledge/tree/HEAD/upstream/FineComputer14451__Grok-Imagine-Cinematic-Studio/.grok/skills/assembly-editor) · ★56)
- **assembly-error-classification-and-counting**: Use when comparing de novo assembler releases (e.g., SOAPdenovo2 vs SOAPdenovo1.05) on GAGE-style benchmark datasets (S. aureus, R. sphaeroides, human chr14) and you need per-error-type counts and correct assembly length metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-error-classification-and-counting))
- **assembly-error-count-normalization**: Use when you have CRAQ output files (locER_out/out_final.CRE.bed, strER_out/out_final.CSE.bed) plus assembly size and need to compute normalized error densities, R-AQI/S-AQI scores, and reference/high/draft/low quality classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-error-count-normalization))
- **assembly-error-rate-assessment**: Use when comparing de novo/hybrid assemblers on identical read datasets and you need error-rate evidence (misassemblies, mismatch/indel rates) plus contiguity (NGA50, genome fraction) and resource cost (runtime, peak memory) per tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-error-rate-assessment))
- **assembly-error-rate-quantification**: Use when comparing de novo assembler versions or assemblies on a reference-backed benchmark and you need continuity (N50) plus per-error-class rates (structural, substitution, copy number) to claim improvement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-error-rate-quantification))
- **assembly-error-vs-heterozygous-loci-classification**: Use when evaluating a de novo genome assembly (e.g. HiCanu contigs of a heterozygous F1 hybrid) where clipped alignment signals must be split into true misassemblies versus haplotype differences before computing assembly quality indexes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-error-vs-heterozygous-loci-classification))
- **assembly-filter-ablation-comparison**: Use when you must quantify how an assembly-stage filter (e.g. Plass's --filter-proteins neural network) changes the retained sequence set, by comparing filtered vs unfiltered candidate ORFs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-filter-ablation-comparison))
- **assembly-fundamentals**: Use when applying assembly fundamentals. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/assembly-fundamentals) · ★2)
- **assembly-fundamentals-and-isas**: Use when deciding whether to write assembly at all, learning or comparing instruction sets, or reasoning about what the machine actually does beneath the ISA. Covers the machine model (registers, addressing modes, flags, endianness, alignment, memory ordering), x86-64 (register file, the instruction set honestly, compiler idioms, the x86 tax), AArch64/ARM64, RISC-V (design philosophy, profiles and fragmentation), and other ISAs worth knowing. Includes the router for the whole assembly-programming reference. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/assembly-programming/skills/assembly-fundamentals-and-isas) · ★5)
- **assembly-gap-filling**: Use when a draft long-read assembly (e.g. FASTA with N-gaps from Flye) should be scaffolded with long reads and have its Ns replaced by spanning read sequence, to improve N50 and gap-free contiguity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-gap-filling))
- **assembly-gap-filling-with-raw-read-sequence**: Use when a long-read-scaffolded assembly contains runs of ambiguous Ns at contig joins and you want to replace those scaffold gaps with actual raw read sequence, then quantify how well gaps were filled. - **assembly-genome-fraction-evaluation**: Use when comparing de novo metagenomic assemblers on a community with known reference genomes and you need quantitative genome recovery (genome fraction %) and contiguity metrics to rank assemblers. - **assembly-graph-tip-and-bubble-simplification**: Use when building a long-read layout backbone from DBG contig-compressed reads (e.g., PacBio reads compressed by SparseAssembler contigs) and the overlap graph must be pruned of contained nodes, tips, and bubbles before consensus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-graph-tip-and-bubble-simplification))
- **assembly-graph-tip-clipping**: Use when assembling full-length isoforms from barcoded RNA-seq linked-read data reference-free and per-barcode subgraphs contain short dead-end or dead-start edges, or tips with low barcode-specific coverage, that must be clipped before bulge removal and gap closing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/assembly-graph-tip-clipping))
- **assembly-graph-variation-representation**: Use when assembling metagenomic or mock-community sequence data (e.g. the Mock B 12-virus community from SRR961514) where strain-level diversity should be preserved and evaluated against alternative assemblers such as MEGAHIT or Ray Meta. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-graph-variation-representation))
- **assembly-graph-visualization**: Use when an assembly step (e.g. Unicycler hybrid, short-read-only, or Nanopore long-read-only assembly of plasmid reads) has produced a graph/FASTG-type assembly output that must be visually inspected for contig structure and circular plasmid structure. - **assembly-metrics-reporting**: Use when you need to quantify inconsistent-overlap reduction between PECAT's first-round (haplotype-collapsed) and second-round (SNP-phased) string graphs on diploid long-read datasets, e.g. to validate that phasing-based overlap filtering worked. - **assembly-misassembly-quantification**: Use when a metagenomic assembly attributes many misassembled blocks to a genome sequenced at ultra-high coverage (&gt;1000x) and you need to test whether excess coverage, not biology, caused the errors. - **assembly-quality-assessment**: Use when preprocessed paired-end metagenomic reads must be de novo assembled and the resulting contigs must be quality-checked via contig count, total length (bp), and N50, e.g. to reproduce VIRGO's assembly cohort statistics.
